# Experiment ledger

Arena batches, oldest first. Opponent BARb, map Quicksilver Remake 1.24, game `byar:test` (test-31357) unless stated.
The arena prints a ready-made row (label, commit with `+` if the tree was dirty, opponent, n, result) at the end of each
batch and stores the same data in the batch's `batch.json`; paste the row here and fill in the last two columns.
W-L-T = wins, losses, timeouts; (n) = aborted by engine crash.

**Where the batches are.** Every batch directory named in this ledger is `run/matches/<batch>` in the checkout, except
the 274 batches of 2026-09-19 and 2026-09-20 (directory names `1789841014-trial` through `1789962363-cmd-opus-low-5`,
28 GB: the early arcs through the commander series), which were moved on 2026-09-28 to
`/ceph/christian/within-reason/run/matches-archive/<batch>` when the root filesystem reached 94%. The tools take a
path, so `run/analyze_match.py /ceph/christian/within-reason/run/matches-archive/<batch>/00` reads an archived
batch as before; later moves go to the same archive and get a line here.

| v24-enemy-base-ab | 05244d1 | medium | 24 (A/B) | 11-10-3 | H-MAP-ENEMY-BASE (enemy base = its factories, not the mean of all buildings seen) against the old rule; first batch recording the opponent's opening | With 7-4-1, without 4-6-2. Openings: 19 bot labs, 5 vehicle plants |
| commander-5-tempo-brief | 40d2e56 | medium, NW, Sonnet field commander | 1, stopped by hand at 37 min (far ahead, not won) | 0-0-1 | field brief, tempo and hidden-information sections, curves line, honest-floor score line | Army 11,200 v 4,100 at minute 24 and 19,400 v 2,800 at minute 33, extractors 6-9 against 9-16 all game, and no kill. Three harness faults: "soldiers seen and not seen dead" never forgot units that died out of sight (14,652 told against 2,800 true), so the commander read 1.4:1 and rationed strikes of 8-20; "free spots on our side" followed a half of the map that shrank as the enemy-base estimate crept toward us (K-maps-our-half-shrinks), and `expansion_radius` could not reach past that half; the commander was never shown where the enemy's factories or commander had been seen, and one strike walked to the game's start guess and found nothing. All three fixed; "ending the game" added to the prompt |

Turns: median 5.2 and 5.9 s, 0.32-0.37 s of thought per game second (0.40 before): half the turns were `orders` then a separate `wait` (`orders` now ends the turn itself). Session restarts work (3 sessions). | commander-8 (three games: radar-start-guess, think-penalty-1, low-effort-penalty-1) | e89c065+ | medium, NW, Sonnet field commander, same seed, all against `corlab` | 1 + 1 + 1 | 0-2-1 (both high-effort games called lost by hand at 11.8 and 16.4 min; the low-effort game timed out at 40.1 min, losing) | radar, start guess beside metal, "the guess is wrong" line; effort high / high with `--think-penalty 1` / low with penalty 1 | Thinking per game second: 0.64 (high), 0.43 (high, penalised), 0.40 (low, penalised); median turn 6.5, 9.0, 6.3 s: most of a turn is tool round trips, not thought. All three sat on one lab and 2-7 extractors. Radar is not why (3-8 towers a game); H-ECO-SPEND fired 0-2 times against 15 in the game-7 win: they never had metal to float because the extractors kept dying. The low-effort game banked 10k of army on 4 extractors, marched on the base at 30:23 with no rockets, arrived strung out (Centurions 350 ahead of the Hammers), lost 4k in 30 s killing the tier-2 lab, then at 32:37, eleven seconds after noting "pressing through", ordered a `move` home: 19 Hammers walked out through Pyros without firing and none arrived. Truth at that moment: 17-19 enemy soldiers, lab dead, nanos dying. Faults: no report line shows what the enemy is losing (the retreat was judged on our losses alone); a withdrawal by `move`; squads do not march together; `to win` fell back to "unscouted" when the only factory seen died |

The best economy and army of the series: 12 extractors at 4:13, 14 at 6:00, 100 soldiers worth 12,000 metal by 18:00 (the user watched it: "we built a big snowball of units, then sat outside the enemy base for minutes"). Jev was ordering the attack; the hands would not walk into a turret's reach by construction. The lane does not touch it. The geometry (the lane-ab records, 258-324 Rocketeer friendly-fire unit-seconds an arm): at the event the Rocketeer's nearest enemy is at a median 404-431 elmos (Rocketeer reach 475, the enemy Hammer 380: it fires from behind the front at what the front fights), a friend of ours stands within 40 of its line to that enemy and nearer than it in 563-1,035 friend-events (Hammers and Rocketeers most), and a friend stands within 60 of the enemy (splash at the impact) in 3-13. So it is the rocket meeting a friend on its low path, not splash at the target; the avoidFriendly refusal does not stop it (the missile is fired past a friend the ray test clears and hits one on the arc, or one that walks in). K-form-rocketeers-hit-the-friends-in-front |

## Fundamentals scorecards (`run/floor.py`, from 2026-09-22)

From the evidence games (2026-09-22 evening) the `base_min` column is `fac_min` (the minute the picture first listed a factory of theirs seen) and `look_min` is measured against the enemy commander's true start from the truth file rather than the bot's guess; the rows above that line keep the old columns (their `look_min` said how soon a unit stood at the guess, which on Comet's standard boxes was 2,000 from the enemy).

From 2026-09-24 three opening columns from `run/replays/assist.py`: `fac4` units out of the factory by 4:00 (the OS 40+ pool's median 19, thebluegecko 24), `stall4` the share of seconds from 1:00 to 4:00 with the metal store below 20 (the pool 9%), `assist4` the commander's seconds guarding the plant by 4:00 (the pool 50, thebluegecko 140); our six games before the brief change: 7, 31%, 0.

The floor of each game, whatever its result (the user: "floor skill: sloppy execution, missed evidence"). Columns:
idle% builder-seconds idle; e0% seconds with the energy store empty; mfull% seconds with the metal store full; react_s
median seconds from a raider at our extractor to an order against it; unanswered such episodes with no order in 60 s;
never orders against a "never" clause of the packet; noop% group asks answering hold on a holding group; illegal policy
orders refused; stuck_s commander move failures up to hands-2, unit-seconds any mobile unit of ours was stuck from yard-1 on (with yard_min, minutes a factory had a stuck unit in its exit lane, in brackets; hands-2 re-read that way: 3,187); known% the player's enemy-army figure over the truth (higher is better);
base_min when the base first read found; look_min when we first stood at their start; turn_s the player's median turn.

The collapse ran 20:00-28:00 (138 losses in 20-24, 72 in 24-28): 182 of 550 losses to something unseen, then Mavericks (67), Pawns (62), Fatboys (37); the player's packets from 19:26 said his E3-E4 nest held Gauntlets that outrange everything and "we do NOT go into E3 or E4", and set never lists on the groups while the extractors fell 32 to 3. The new states: raid offered 14,344 slot-seconds and never picked (its gate noul median 0.10, max 0.43, never over 0.5), sweep 4,372 never picked, pull_out picked 35 times, close_on_shooter 10, gather 3, shell 3, station lists used by the player ("spot_31 spot_37", "spot_9 spot_4"). No bot errors; Jev median 231-240 ms. The pianist's log is 228 MB (49 KB a call): the viewer loads it in headless Chromium, the user's viewer instance was serving an old match dir. Harness faults: the shelling estimate is one for the whole side (weapon by hit count, one place), so at 21:15 every group on the map, group_M at B2 included, was offered `pull_out` from a Beamer (490) at E4 while group_G stood inside a seen Gauntlet's 1,220 and flipped 13 s between pull-out and station 21:17-21:34; `run/floor.py`, `raid_ledger.py`, `hands_window.py` and the Jev replay arms imported the deleted jev_audit.py (fixed: `run/match_read.py`). The raid states targeted the nest the packet forbade (spot_21, spot_37, spot_32, spot_20: 10.5k of the 14.3k state-seconds) or extractors 2,900-3,800 away: declining them was the gate working. | player-24-hunts | ccc7858 | **BARb medium**, Comet Catcher, mirror, Opus 5.5 medium, think penalty 1 cap 7, seed 1, claude2 (`run/matches/1790657006-player-24-hunts`) | 1 | 0-0-1 (called by the user at 8:34, behind) | Hunts as groups of their own, the planner retired (no-live-heuristics stages 5-6). | CALLED at 8:34 by the user ("a group of rovers rushed in and is killing us, and we organized no response"): extractors ours/his 6/5 at 4:00, 13/8 at 7:00, 8/9 at 8:00; army 533/660 at 4:00, 990/2,599 at 8:00; 14 extractors lost, 2,371 metal lost at our base. The cause is a contradiction between the hands and the engine: the hands offer a hunt by the group's fastest armed members when none outrun the quarry ("without catching it: it leaves when they arrive, or stands and dies", the onepass-player-4 lesson), and the engine drops every hunter whose speed is not above the quarry's ("slower") in the hunt's first tick, so the hunt ends "after 0 s: no hunters": 46 of 47 hunt ends this game (a Blitz at 101 against a Blitz at 101 or a Rover at 168; a Rover against a Rover). Each picked hunt split its hunters off (12 splits: group_C at 2:50, D 5:31, E 6:38, F/G/H 6:48-6:50, I 7:03, J/K 7:10-7:11, L 7:13) and the hunt's end left them holding as a group of one: ten singleton groups at 7:38, 990 metal of Blitzes and Rovers within 1,100 of home, when his 8 Blitzes and a Rover (911) drove into the base. The player saw it at 6:59 ("our Blitzes split into 5 single detachments by failing hunts") and wrote "every one of our small groups joins group_A" at 6:59, 7:18 ("no hunts, no detachments"), 7:37 and 7:47 ("attack party_10 at home now, with the whole group"), 8:13 ("each joins group_F now"); Jev kept picking hunts (7:19-7:36, at 0.25-0.59) and picked one join at 8:26 (group_A into group_F, 0.2). The join state is offered only into the nearest group of the domain (plan.rs §5), so "join group_A" was never on the board for most groups, and it rated 0.14-0.3 throughout; a whole-group attack by a group of one against 8 Blitzes rated 0.07-0.2, rightly. The pass picks one changed actor a second, so massing ten groups needs ten picks at best. The 8:04 note: "HANDS FAULT ... Wish: a direct order to a group (attack party_X) that bypasses the hands." Fixed after (the user): the engine's slower and hurt drops deleted (H-MICRO-HUNT), the merge words say the one body (H-HANDS-GROUPS). claude2 7-day +0.0 points. |
